SYSTEM INTEGRATION ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW:
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Each n8n workflow listed below has been ingested, parsed, mapped, and wired into SARA's unified
Python and Node.js execution engine (`sara_automation_suite.py` + `dist/server.cjs`).
Key execution mechanisms:
  1. Native Fast-Path: Bypasses external n8n runtime overhead by executing directly via win32 API,
     PyAutoGUI, SQLite FTS5, Requests, BeautifulSoup, and FastAPI.
  2. Gemini Live Session Dispatch: Exposed as real-time callable tools to SARA's voice core.
  3. CLI Interface: Callable from terminal via `python sara_automation_suite.py n8n_execute <name>`.
  4. Antigravity IDE Bridge: Exportable as structured autonomous development tasks into Antigravity.
  5. Verified Safe Routing: Strict real credentials only (+923332656733, pashadev804@gmail.com).
